carrier_cost = carrier_cost_by_type[carrier_type]\n\n        reagent_price_per_1000 = _to_decimal(a.get(\"reagent_price_per_1000_tests_usd\"))\n        tests_small = int(a.get(\"tests_per_lab_per_run_small\"))\n        tests_bulk = int(a.get(\"tests_per_lab_per_run_bulk\"))\n\n        runs_small = 24\n        runs_bulk = 12\n\n        annual_revenue_small = payment * Decimal(active_labs) * Decimal(runs_small)\n        annual_revenue_bulk = payment * Decimal(active_labs) * Decimal(runs_bulk)\n\n        annual_reagent_cost_small = (\n            reagent_price_per_1000\n            * Decimal(active_labs)\n            * Decimal(tests_small)\n            * Decimal(runs_small)\n            / Decimal(\"1000\")\n        )\n        annual_reagent_cost_bulk = (\n            reagent_price_per_1000\n            * Decimal(active_labs)\n            * Decimal(tests_bulk)\n            * Decimal(runs_bulk)\n            / Decimal(\"1000\")\n        )\n\n        # Carrier cost interpretation: per-shipment (per lab per run)\n        annual_carrier_cost_small = carrier_cost * Decimal(active_labs) * Decimal(runs_small)\n        annual_carrier_cost_bulk = carrier_cost * Decimal(active_labs) * Decimal(runs_bulk)\n\n        annual_margin_small = annual_revenue_small - annual_reagent_cost_small - annual_carrier_cost_small\n        annual_margin_bulk = annual_revenue_bulk - annual_reagent_cost_bulk - annual_carrier_cost_bulk\n        annual_diff = annual_margin_bulk - annual_margin_small\n\n        total_small += annual_margin_small\n        total_bulk += annual_margin_bulk\n        total_diff += annual_diff\n\n        per_assay_entries.append(\n            {\n                \"assay_id\": assay_id,\n \"annual_revenue_bulk_kit_usd\": _usd2f(annual_revenue_bulk),\n                \"annual_reagent_cost_small_kit_usd\": _usd2f(annual_reagent_cost_small),\n _usd2f(total_diff),\n        \"absolute_total_margin_difference_usd\": _usd2f(abs(total_diff)),\n    }\n\n    abs_diff = abs(_usd2(total_diff))\n    threshold = Decimal(\"7000\")\n    if abs_diff < threshold:\n        decision = \"adopt_bulk_kit\"\n        justification = (\n            f\"Absolute total annual margin difference (${_usd2(abs_diff)}) is below \"\n            f\"the switch
